# 1b93461 Add gitkb notes for master's PRs #2-#5 and the submodule move

## Summary
Commits gitkb notes (5 commit notes and their change notes) for fa4f8c2, 8dc875c, 7206674, af05a03 and 4bc8390, written through the MCP tools served from the jobseekers-mcp submodule.

## Why
Keeps the knowledge base current with master's PRs #2-#5 and the move of the MCP server to its own repository.

## Risks
none identified; the files are generated and named by the sha256 of the summarized input.

## Commit message
```text
Add gitkb notes for master's PRs #2-#5 and the submodule move

Summarized through the MCP tools, now served from the jobseekers-mcp
submodule: fa4f8c2 (PR #2: gitkb, regions, visa), 8dc875c (PR #3: Langfuse
best practices in jobagent), 7206674 (PR #4: six new job-board sources and
board detection), af05a03 (PR #5: MCP server) and 4bc8390 (MCP server moved
to its own repo).

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>
```
